PID regional buses · Mladoboleslavsko (okres Mladá Boleslav) · Monday 2 Nov 2026

PID Mladoboleslavsko

Every PID bus line of okres Mladá Boleslav (licences 26xxxx) and the Prague and other lines whose outer end lies here, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. PID Mladoboleslavsko: okres Mladá Boleslav (licences 260xxx), the Prague lines whose outer end is here (315, 345, 403, 412) and the long lines of the Liberecký and Královéhradecký kraj placed here (405, 427, 700, 720, 721, 722, 723, 730, 733, 735, 540345); PID GTFS of 1 Nov 2026, Monday 2 Nov 2026 (a school day). Every terminus is treated as a loop (typed = real − 1); the 0-minute turns of 431 Stará Lysá and 733 are modelled as in the timetable. Most district lines are school-day lines of single trips, each bent onto one real afternoon round on one bus (every 120 to 480); nine lines run every 480 for stops no other line serves, inside or outside the kraj (403, 405 Vrchlabí, 431 Stará Lysá, 540345, 700, 714 Sobotka, 720 Semily, 725 Vlčí Pole, 730); Dětenice, Brodek is the last leg of the 735 circuit. Hand-set model hours (anchor): 315, 345, 412 and 714 at 16:00 (their busier hours are short trips over their own stops), 738 at 16:00 (its busier hours are the Dolní Bukovina short and the 13:15 / 13:50 pair, whose minutes miss its two-hourly pattern); 427, 716, 719 and 726 at 15:30, 768 at 15:00 and 431, 735 and 767 at 14:30, where their one-bus rounds run. Praha, Černý Most towards Turnov has a departure every hour at :15 from 720 (13:15, 14:15, 17:15, 18:15), 700 (15:15) and 730 (16:15). Through-running in the timetable: 699 → 769 at Chotětov and 734 → 733 at Sezemice (both modelled as one Simutrans line), 733 Turnov – Vlastibořice (modelled as a circuit), 732 → 737 at Čistá, hřiště (3 of 3), 726 → 766 at Březno, 767 → 699 at Bezno (4 of 8), 345 ↔ 540345 at Liberec and Mladá Boleslav (2 of 3); at Mnichovo Hradiště most regional departures (716, 717, 738) leave right after a 315 arrival. Open for the 2 Nov live map: whether the buses of 315, 345 and 405 turn at Praha, Černý Most in 0 minutes (the arrival minute is the departure minute; the sheet keeps at least 3 min, which costs 315 one bus, 345 one and 405 one), and the pairings 732 ↔ 737 at Čistá, hřiště (3 of 3), 767 → 699 at Bezno (4 of 8), 737 → 775 at Mladá Boleslav (5 of 15 departures), 467 → 699 (7 of 9) and 467 → 724 (5 of 10) at Mladá Boleslav, 713 ↔ 718 at Sobotka (2 of 6).

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot are off the pattern, in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal. Real buses swap lines at the terminals, so per-line totals are higher than what the operators run; pooling lines is the owner's choice and is not done.

In the game. Each card compares the save's line (export of 30 Oct 2026) with the modelled stops. The save's buses are shown as a check; the sheet is built from the real timetable and the buses seen running, so a game line may need buses added or moved.

To check

Choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative; then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off.

Regional PID buses use the VZ-PID-bus objects, town lines the VZ-MHD<Town> ones (pak rules); objects are read from the installed pak. "requested" marks a type with no object yet (pak_request.md).

How this was built

Scope. PID GTFS downloaded Sun 1 Nov 2026 (feed 1-14 Nov). Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (the first live-map poll day), a normal school weekday (28 Oct was a holiday and 29-30 Oct autumn school holidays; regional lines run many school-day-only trips). The Středočeský kraj licenses each district's lines with its own prefix (26xxxx = okres Mladá Boleslav); a line licensed by Prague or by another kraj is on the sheet of the okres of its outer end (the first stop, coming in from the far end, in a Středočeský municipality), or, with no Prague end, of the okres most of its stops lie in. 48 lines: 45 modelled here, 0 on the Prague bus sheet, 3 listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v4): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from the knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand (method-check file in the pipeline folder).

Buses. PID live map (mapa.pid.cz getData.php), polled every minute 12:00-20:00 (every 3 min 5:00-12:00 and 20:00-23:00) on Mon 2 Nov 2026, and on Tue 3 Nov 2026 so far only 5:00-7:20 (this version, 3 Nov morning: the Tuesday trips fill only designed buses and missing types; the evening version adds the whole Tuesday), by scripts/01_live_poll.py; each bus once per line through getVehicleWindow.php (operator, type, course). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching, across all twelve oblast sheets; buses on the Prague bus sheet are not used here.

Sources: PID GTFS, PID live map, the save-game export of 30 Oct 2026 (engine session).
